# Anki estate reorganization plan

> **Study only.** This proposal and its draft scripts operate on disposable copies of an automatic Anki backup. They are deliberately incapable of targeting the live syllabus profile. Nothing in this commission authorizes a live collection change, pipeline deployment, add-on edit, media deletion, or AnkiWeb sync.
>
> **Live execution was separately owner-authorized on 2026-08-07** under the conditions and mechanism recorded in [§ Live copy-back cutover](#live-copy-back-cutover-owner-authorized-2026-08-07) below. The script guards remain unchanged: the live collection file is never a script target.

## Recommendation

Adopt six deterministic language roots—`DE German`, `ES Spanish`, `FR French`, `IT Italian`, `PT Portuguese`, and `ZH Mandarin`—and the same numbered study lanes inside the five active European-language roots. Move the 20,513 compatible `YouTube Expression Pool v1` fluency cards into the active Fluency lanes. Archive and suspend the 6,191 superseded Cloud/Idiom/raw-phrase tasks intact, leaving Expression Focus empty for fresh `Idiomatic Expression Hub v1` cards. Keep native-language syllabus labels below the numbered lanes, place each Pimsleur course with its language, and move inactive or retired material under `zz Dormant`.

The estate migration itself should change no note model, note GUID, note field, card identity, due date, interval, FSRS state, or review row. It should only:

- create empty target decks;
- add provenance and surface-collision evidence tags;
- change card deck IDs through Anki's public `set_deck` API;
- rename the Mandarin and Pimsleur source subtrees through Anki's public deck API so their deck IDs and metadata survive;
- suspend explicitly retired old expression tasks, long-audio cards, and an experiment if the recommended decision is approved; and
- remove source deck rows only after they are empty.

Exact bilingual surface equality is evidence, not canonical identity. The recommended collision policy is therefore **defer to the sense-resolved Expression Hub manifest**: phase 7 only adds reversible group tags and never picks a winner, moves a schedule, or suspends another card. The recommended `EXPERIMENTS-YT` decision is to suspend and demote its 27 `YouTube Audio Phrase v3` cards because their target-language/audio-to-English task is not compatible with the active English-to-target Fluency task. Media remains in place.

These scripts are a copy-only estate rehearsal, not a standalone production cutover. Any later authorized collection change must fold the reviewed estate placements into Expression Hub migration phase 5 after canonical expression/example IDs, sense decisions, models `1820180001`/`1820180002`, and the complete asset manifest are ready. Running this draft independently on the live profile would create an unsafe gap between retired tasks and their ID-bound replacements.

This plan was generated from the newest automatic backup available for the study:

The source-of-truth factual audit is [ANKI_ESTATE_INVENTORY.md](ANKI_ESTATE_INVENTORY.md). The pristine working database is read with SQLite `mode=ro&immutable=1`; the scripts now refuse a nonempty WAL or journal sidecar because immutable reads would otherwise be capable of seeing a stale main file. Mutation rehearsals must start from a separate byte-for-byte clone beneath the same work directory.

This 12:47 estate snapshot supersedes the 10:18 observational collection counts quoted in the current Expression Hub study. The Hub's phase-0 contract already requires a fresh joint census; any production event must regenerate both manifests from one post-sync copy rather than mixing either document's observed counts.

## Invariants

The following are release-blocking, not best-effort checks:

1. Notes remain exactly 75,174; cards remain exactly 83,139; revlog remains exactly 62,554; mature cards remain exactly 4,639; total reps remain exactly 61,435.
2. The ordered hashes of note identity, note field content, complete note-model/field/template schema, scheduling fields, and revlog rows must equal the phase-1 baseline. Deck IDs and queues are excluded only where the plan explicitly changes them.
3. The pre-existing eight suspended Mandarin Props cards remain suspended. With the recommended decisions, the only new suspensions are 6,191 superseded expression tasks, 12,667 retired-audio cards, and 27 experimental Phrase-v3 cards, for an expected final suspended total of 18,893. Phase 7 adds evidence tags but changes no queue.
4. Lex-Stage remains the same 18 notes and 28 cards in the same deck; the verifier must compare its exact card/deck membership fingerprint, not counts alone.
5. No note model is converted. No note or card is deleted. No revlog row is reassigned or synthesized.
6. The set of note field media references is unchanged. Tags may change only through journaled additions; queues may change only for journaled suspension IDs.
7. Mandarin's internal suffix hierarchy remains identical. Only its common parent changes.

Every `2 Expression Focus` shell is reserved for fresh model `1820180001`; no old Cloud, Idiom, or raw-phrase card may occupy it. The five initially empty `Diagnosed trouble spots` shells reflect the four Expression Hub choices the owner accepted on 2026-08-07; this estate rehearsal creates only their deck rows and no diagnosis cards.

## Migration mechanics by estate family

| Family | Observed scope | Proposed action | Exact mechanism | Principal risk and control |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| Compatible active Fluency | 20,513 cards/notes from 10 current source decks | Merge into five language Fluency destinations | Add provenance first, then `Collection.set_deck(card_ids, target_did)` | Only `YouTube Expression Pool v1` has the compatible active task direction. Per-video provenance would vanish if phase 2 were skipped; phase 3 refuses apply without its predecessor and a frozen collision manifest. |
| Superseded expression tasks | 6,191 cards/notes from 425 current source decks | Archive into five language buckets and suspend intact | `Collection.set_deck`, then scheduler `suspend_cards` | Includes Cloud/Idiom hubs, Reverse v1, Phrase v3, and the 236 cards already under `Idiomatic::z-archive`; actual Hub phase 5 may convert only manifest-validated compatible predecessors. This rehearsal never guesses. |
| Grammar, tenses, exercises, translation, errors, rescue | 5,391 cards from 121 source decks | Move under numbered language lanes while retaining native suffixes | `Collection.set_deck`; no note-model conversion | Future APKGs can recreate old roots unless all builders change before polling resumes. |
| Mandarin families | 4,111 cards across eight source roots | Place beneath `ZH Mandarin`, internals untouched | Rename each source subtree root in place | Creating the destination first would force a conflict; phase 1 deliberately does not precreate these rename destinations. Phase 5 fingerprints deck IDs plus `common`/`kind` metadata. |
| Pimsleur | 34,211 cards across eleven language roots | Place active courses with language; demote four inactive languages | Rename each language subtree root in place | A card-by-card move would discard source deck metadata. The rename journal records the source deck ID and every expected descendant destination. |
| `z-archive` | 236 cards in 22 source decks | Collapse to four observed language archive buckets under dormant | Included in phase 3's old-task move; phase 5 retains an idempotent residual `set_deck` fallback | The ordered rehearsal has zero residual archive moves in phase 5. Nothing is promoted from archive by surface equality. |
| Long Idioms Audio | 12,667 cards across 6,663 notes in 17 source decks | Move to ten dormant direction decks and suspend | `set_deck`, then scheduler `suspend_cards` | Suspension is an intentional queue change; every affected pre-state is journaled. Fields, notes, and media references remain. |
| Exact bilingual surface collisions | 2,646 groups covering 5,297 candidate notes | Add one reversible `estate::surface_collision::<group-id>` tag to every candidate note, or do nothing under `keep-all` | Tag-only phase; no winner, loser, card move, or queue change | Surface plus gloss still does not establish expression/sense identity. The later ID-bound Hub manifest resolves or quarantines each candidate without merging revlog. |
| `EXPERIMENTS-YT::Ciumes do Uber` | 27 Phrase-v3 cards, 43 reps | Recommended suspend and demote under `zz Dormant::Experiments` | `set_deck`, then scheduler `suspend_cards` | Owner-gated. `keep` records a no-op phase. Creating active EN-to-target replacements belongs to the sense-resolved Hub manifest, not this estate guess. |
| Empty obsolete shells | Every source row left empty after phases 1–8 | Remove deepest first | Public deck removal only after a fresh zero-direct-card query | Phase 9 refuses if any candidate contains a card and journals each exact legacy deck record before its first removal. |

### Why no note-model change belongs in this migration

Deck placement is card-level in Anki. A note may legitimately project cards into more than one deck; the copied collection already has two cross-deck notes. Changing a card's deck therefore preserves the note, GUID, fields, template/model, schedule, and revlog. Converting a note model is materially different: it can change card templates and card ordinals, create or destroy cards, and complicate GUID/update behavior. The estate plan requires **zero** such conversions.

The estate rehearsal itself performs zero conversions. The accepted Expression Hub migration is the sole schema window: it installs frozen models `1820180001` and `1820180002`, converts only manifest-compatible active predecessors, and creates ID-derived replacements. Its phase 5 must incorporate these estate placements in the same controlled collection transaction. This tree reserves the destinations: canonical example projections live in `1 Expressions::1 Fluency`, fresh hub projections live in `1 Expressions::2 Expression Focus`, and accepted Flag-1 diagnoses live in `4 Exercises::Diagnosed trouble spots`. Images, source contexts, and future top-up examples remain note data, not deck dimensions.

## Per-video deck elimination and provenance

Phase 2 runs before any source deck name disappears. For each qualifying video-source note it preserves existing tags and adds the missing subset of:

- `youtube`;
- `lang::<de|es|fr|it|pt>`;
- `source::youtube::<11-character-id>` when a `Source` field contains a recognizable YouTube URL; and
- `estate::origin::<12-hex>` where the suffix is the first 12 hexadecimal characters of SHA-1 over the exact current deck name.

The phase would add 9,894 tag assignments to 3,298 notes, comprising 837 distinct added tags and 420 origin mappings. The journal stores the complete `estate::origin::* → original deck name` legend, so the short stable tag is reversible without putting a long, punctuation-heavy title into Anki's tag namespace. Existing source fields, video URLs, video IDs, titles, and tags remain unchanged.

This is intentionally additive. It does not infer a video ID from an arbitrary 11-character tag unless the source evidence supports it, and it does not invent a provenance value for notes that never belonged to a video-source deck.

## Surface-collision policy

The automated evidence boundary is deliberately narrow: same language, same representation, and exact normalized target **and** English text. Normalization strips HTML and `[sound:]`, unescapes HTML, applies Unicode NFKC and stable quote/dash forms, collapses whitespace, and case-folds; it preserves accents and punctuation.

The copied collection contains 2,646 exact surface groups covering 5,297 notes. All are source-versus-pool groups; four also cross the legacy/Idiomatic generation boundary. They are not automatically actionable duplicates. Equal surface and gloss can still represent distinct senses, source occurrences, task directions, or durable example identities.

The recommended `defer-to-hub-manifest` policy gives each group a reversible `estate::surface_collision::<group-id>` tag and changes nothing else. There is no winner/loser ranking, schedule promotion, queue change, note deletion, or revlog transplant. Phase 3 has already archived and suspended every old-task candidate intact. The accepted Expression Hub's collection-checksummed manifest must later resolve each note against durable expression/example IDs, sense keys, context, task compatibility, and schedule evidence; ambiguity is quarantined. `keep-all` is the no-tag alternative.

Twenty-four strict target-only candidates and one punctuation-relaxed candidate remain separate manual-review evidence, not automatic mutations. A visually similar expression is not enough to prove that two bilingual learning prompts are interchangeable.

## Media policy

The backup contained no media payload, so the copied database alone cannot prove that a live media file is orphaned. Its notes reference 138,114 unique filenames in 148,597 occurrences. The earlier name-and-`stat`-only media scan, tied to the 10:18 collection snapshot rather than this 12:47 snapshot, estimated 158,131 files / 6.482 GiB as an **orphanable historical upper bound**, not a current deletion list; 7,037 collision-suffix files / 1.628 GiB deserve extra caution. The newer copy has a net 560 additional reference names, but the underlying added/removed set can make the historical candidate set shrink or grow; no exact revised orphan count is claimed without a new copied-media scan.

One copied-DB reference was absent from the inspected media directory: `\-2qnze5hq.webm`, used by a Mandarin Props note whose card has a 2,163-day interval and 28 reps. That is a repair candidate, never a reason to delete the note or card.

Therefore:

1. The estate migration performs no media write, rename, deduplication, or deletion.
2. Suspended audio/old-task notes and surface-collision candidates still count as references; “not actively studied” does not mean orphaned.
3. After an approved migration, compare the pre/post ordered media-reference fingerprint; it must be identical because note fields do not change.
4. Any later cleanup requires a copied media directory, Anki's own media check, content hashes rather than basename assumptions, quarantine instead of immediate deletion, and a separate owner approval after the missing Mandarin reference is repaired.

## Pipeline and add-on compatibility

Renaming the collection while old publishers or pending APKGs remain active is unsafe: the next import can recreate an obsolete tree. Expression publication must switch through the accepted Hub's ID-based snapshot/delta cutover; the remaining grammar, exercise, translation, tense, rescue, Pimsleur, and Mandarin producers must switch their baked deck names inside the same paused maintenance window.

Create one shared source of truth such as `anki_root(lang)` returning `DE German`, `ES Spanish`, `FR French`, `IT Italian`, or `PT Portuguese`; builders should compose paths from it. The estate rehearsal changes no model or GUID. The joint production cutover intentionally has one separately controlled schema window for new models `1820180001`/`1820180002` and ID-derived Hub/Example GUIDs. Where package families write the same destination, their deck-ID formulas should be centralized or covered by an import test proving that Anki converges by deck name.

| Producer / consumer | Current baked name or behavior | Required target contract |
|---|---|---|
| `idiomatic/worker.py::process_video` | `Idiomatic::<English language>::<date> · <title>` | Retire the per-video APKG/deck path. Persist source occurrences, title, URL, and context against durable server IDs; project only canonical Hub/Example snapshot or delta cards. |
| `idiomatic/pipeline/pool.py::_build_expression_pool` | `Idiomatic::<English language>::Fluency Expressions` | Retire after the transition snapshot. The replacement ID-based Example builder publishes canonical examples to `<ROOT>::1 Expressions::1 Fluency`. |
| `idiomatic/pipeline/pool.py::_build_idioms_pool` | `Idiomatic::<English language>::Idioms` | Retire. The replacement ID-based Hub builder creates fresh model `1820180001` cards in `<ROOT>::1 Expressions::2 Expression Focus`. |
| `idiomatic/pipeline/pool.py::_build_idiom_audio_pool` | `Idiomatic::<English language>::Idioms Audio (…)` | Keep generation off (`build_audio_pools=False`), retire the builder, and stop offering any stale unacknowledged `pool_idiom_t2e/e2t` rows. Do not generate new dormant cards. |
| `idiomatic/grammar/apkg.py::deck_name_for` | `Idiomatic Grammar {LANG}::{cluster}` | Normal clusters: `<ROOT>::2 Grammar::{cluster}`. A cluster beginning `9 ` routes to `<ROOT>::6 My Errors` (only a deeper suffix, if any, is retained). |
| `idiomatic/api.py::admin_grammar_deckmap` | Reuses grammar `deck_name_for` | It must return the same new paths; this is what the add-on's tag-based organizer consumes. |
| `idiomatic/grammar/podcast_cards.py` | Calls grammar `deck_name_for` | It follows the new `2 Grammar::0 …` path through that shared helper. |
| `idiomatic/grammar/exercises2.py::deck_name_for` | `Idiomatic Exercises {LANG}::{topic label}` | `<ROOT>::4 Exercises::{topic label}`. |
| New Expression Hub diagnosis release path | No current producer | Publish accepted Flag-1 diagnosis cards only to `<ROOT>::4 Exercises::Diagnosed trouble spots`; do not route those reviews into expression-weakness telemetry. |
| `idiomatic/grammar/translation.py::deck_name_for` | `Idiomatic Translation {LANG}::{cluster}` | `<ROOT>::5 Translation::{cluster}`. |
| `idiomatic/grammar/tenses.py::deck_name_for` | `Idiomatic Tenses {LANG}::{verb}` or `Idiomatic Tenses Exercises {LANG}::{verb}` | Production: `<ROOT>::3 Tenses::1 Production::{verb}`; exercises: `<ROOT>::3 Tenses::2 Exercises::{verb}`. |
| External rescue-comics APKG producer, staged by `POST /admin/apkg-upload` as `rescue_comics` | Current collection uses `Idiomatic Rescue Comics::{LANG}`; Asset Factory strategy also mentions `Idiomatic Rescue::<Lang>` | `<ROOT>::7 Rescue`; the upload endpoint itself does not rewrite an APKG's internal deck name, so the external builder is the required change point. |
| `idiomatic/api.py` video-purge cleanup response | Reconstructs the old per-video deck name and returns it with note GUIDs | Replace deck deletion with source-ID disposition. Removing a video must retain the expression, canonical examples, assets, and shared Hub/Example cards; never put a shared target deck in `cleanup.json`. |
| Local Idiomatic add-on `_reorganize_existing_decks` | Can rename per-video decks back into `Idiomatic::<Language>::<date> · title` | Retire/disable this legacy action. A new estate version marker must not reuse `grammar_reorg.done.json`; the existing grammar organizer is the safe `col.set_deck` precedent. |
| Add-on `/apkgs/pending` import | Imports every unacked package for the profile and APKG names are authoritative for new cards | Add release kind, manifest sequence, and profile-schema gates; block retired audio, old pool/per-video packages, and every pre-cutover package during the maintenance window. Resume only ID-based full snapshots/deltas plus renamed non-expression families. |
| `/home/admin/projects/pimsleur/scraper/build_apkg.py` | `Pimsleur::<Language>::Level N` | Active roots route to each language's `8 Pimsleur`; inactive roots route to `zz Dormant::Pimsleur`; Spanish variants retain `Spain` / `Latin America`. |
| Mandarin external builders in `/home/admin/projects/mandarin-videos` | Bake `Mandarin Props`, `Mandarin Palace::*`, `Mandarin Actors`, `Mandarin Locations`, `Mandarin Zones`, `Mandarin China Provinces`, and dated Mandarin Characters names | Prefix only with `ZH Mandarin::`; retain all internal suffixes and stable deck IDs. Direct deck-ID readers continue to work because phase 5 preserves IDs, but name-based readers/builders must be audited. |

Dashboard display labels and documentation should be updated in the same pipeline release, but they are not collection-safety dependencies. Historical database fields that record the old deck-at-adoption may remain as provenance rather than being rewritten.

## Safe cutover sequence for a future, separately approved migration

1. **Freeze the accepted contracts in code.** Record the estate decisions and the four accepted Hub verdicts, centralize all target deck names, retire old expression/per-video/audio publishers, implement the Hub's ID-based snapshots/deltas and source-safe purge semantics, and test exact deck/model/GUID/release manifests. Deployment gates must keep all new writers inactive.
2. **Drain, sync, then freeze.** While the old tree is still authoritative, import only legitimate old pending packages, explicitly exclude retired audio, complete an ordinary sync, and confirm no failed/retry delivery remains. Then stop publication and polling, close or quarantine every other Anki client, and require zero candidate cards with `odid` or `odue` set.
3. **Create the authoritative post-sync evidence copy.** Take a fresh backup/copy; regenerate the full estate inventory, surface-collision evidence, Hub sense/ID/task-direction manifest, media and scheduling fingerprints, and owner decision hashes from that exact state. The August 7 study copy proves the method only. Any later review, edit, flag, or sync invalidates the cutover baseline.
4. **Finish Hub phases 1–3 before collection mutation.** Stage the canonical server schema and durable IDs, resolve or quarantine every source/example/sense, finish the complete approved asset set, build the two frozen models and target manifests, and replay the full joint payload twice on disposable copies. Run these estate phases 1–10 and the complete newest-to-oldest rollback against the same frozen copy as a required sub-rehearsal.
5. **Use the Hub's single schema window.** With clients and imports still paused, checkpoint the authoritative desktop and run Hub phase 4 to install models `1820180001`/`1820180002` and stable target decks. Do not attempt an ordinary multi-client sync mid-migration.
6. **Execute one integrated Hub phase 5.** A separately reviewed production executor—not these copy-guarded drafts—converts only manifest-compatible fluency predecessors, creates missing ID-derived Example and Hub cards, archives every incompatible old task, and applies the remaining approved estate placements. There is no interval in which old tasks are retired live without their validated replacements ready.
7. **Validate, bind, and cut over publication.** Complete Hub phase 6 media/hash/render/idempotency checks, stage exact collection bindings, then perform the controlled full upload and ID-based pipeline cutover in Hub phase 7. Only after acknowledgements and set-equality checks pass may polling resume.
8. **Prove no obsolete tree can return.** Import one release of every allowed rolling kind, reject a deliberately stale/pre-cutover release, and assert that no `Idiomatic*`, `Languages::*`, top-level `Pimsleur`, or old Mandarin root is recreated. Keep checkpoints and journals through at least one full desktop/mobile review cycle.

## Rehearsal phases, pause points, and rollback

Every phase has a dry run, requires its predecessor's completed journal on apply, and uses an atomically replaced, fsynced JSON journal beneath the selected copy tree.

| Phase | Work on the copy | Pause/verification gate | Local rollback |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 0 | Validate path, SQLite header and sidecars; inventory filtered-deck state and gate every later apply on `odid = odue = 0`; record SHA-256 and full invariants; generate inventory/deck-map reports under `docs/research/` and keep the collision manifest plus owner decisions beside the copy | Source SHA and totals must match the intended backup; decisions must match the hardcoded structural scheme | Discard artifacts; database is untouched |
| 1 | Create 205 missing target shells, including the five accepted diagnosis destinations | No invariant except the deck catalog may change; decision JSON and hash enter the baseline journal; filtered-deck precondition is rechecked before apply | Remove only journaled, still-empty created decks |
| 2 | Add normalized provenance tags | Only note-tag and registered-tag hashes may change; origin legend must be complete | Remove only tags actually added and restore the registered-tag catalog |
| 3 | Move 26,704 cards: 20,513 compatible active Fluency cards to five active lanes; 6,191 old hub/raw-phrase tasks to five archives and suspend them | Manifest identity/content must still match; all cards reach exact journaled destinations; Expression Focus remains free of old models | Restore captured card deck/queue states |
| 4 | Move 5,391 learning-family cards to 121 exact destinations | Schedule, fields, GUIDs, queues, and revlog unchanged | Reverse captured card moves |
| 5 | Rename 19 Mandarin/Pimsleur roots covering 38,322 cards; confirm zero residual archive moves after phase 3 | Every renamed deck ID plus `common`/`kind` metadata and every descendant card destination must match | Reverse any residual archive moves, then reverse root renames using the captured source IDs |
| 6 | Move and suspend 12,667 retired-audio cards | Every intended card is suspended; no other queue changes | Move cards back and unsuspend only IDs newly suspended by this phase |
| 7 | Record 2,646 exact surface groups with 5,297 tag assignments under `defer-to-hub-manifest`, or record an approved `keep-all` no-op | Policy must equal the decision file; cards, queues, schedules, content, and revlog stay fixed | Remove only tags this phase added, or no-op |
| 8 | Recommended: demote and suspend 27 experiment cards; alternative: record an approved `keep` no-op | Journal action must equal the decision file | Reverse the move/suspension, or no-op |
| 9 | Remove only empty obsolete source shells, deepest first | Any nonempty candidate aborts the phase; all exact legacy deck records are captured before deletion starts | Restore exact journaled legacy deck records |
| 10 | Read-only final verification | Exact ordered phase chain, sacred hashes, target/obsolete tree, moved destinations, rename metadata, tag additions, authorized suspensions, decision consistency, Lex membership, no `odid`/`odue` filtered state, and `quick_check=ok` | No mutation |

For a disposable rehearsal, the most reliable whole-run rollback remains deleting the migrated clone and making a new clone from the frozen pristine copy. `rollback.py` is valuable for proving each journal but must run newest-to-oldest. For any future production event, the reliable escape hatch is the fresh cutover backup with package delivery still paused; never attempt an improvised export/reimport or partial AnkiWeb overwrite.

## Acceptance checklist

- [ ] The owner has approved every decision below and the copy-local JSON passes structural validation.
- [ ] Every mutation input, manifest, decision file, and journal is anchored under the selected copy's directory; read-only outputs stay under `docs/research/`; symlink paths and any output alias of a collection inode are refused.
- [ ] The collision manifest was produced from the same pristine database SHA used by phase 1.
- [ ] All phases 1–9 appear exactly once, in order, with a continuous before/after invariant chain.
- [ ] Notes 75,174; cards 83,139; revlog 62,554; mature 4,639; reps 61,435.
- [ ] Note identity/content, complete note-model schema, scheduling-core, revlog, and exact Lex membership hashes match baseline.
- [ ] All moved and renamed cards are in their journaled final decks; all renamed deck IDs and metadata survived.
- [ ] Exactly the authorized suspension set changed; with recommended decisions total suspended is 18,893.
- [ ] No active card uses a retired audio or superseded hub/raw-phrase model, except the 27 experiment cards only if the owner chose `keep`; no old task occupies Expression Focus; every card has `odid = odue = 0`.
- [ ] Required provenance tags are present; surface-collision evidence tags are present under `defer-to-hub-manifest` and absent by design under `keep-all`; no pre-existing tag assignment disappeared.
- [ ] No obsolete source root remains, except `EXPERIMENTS-YT` only if the owner chose `keep`.
- [ ] Lex-Stage, Default, and Custom Study remain; Mandarin suffix structure is unchanged.
- [ ] Media-reference fingerprint is unchanged and the missing mature Mandarin reference is queued for repair, not deletion.
- [ ] A post-cutover import smoke test for every APKG kind creates no old root.

## Decisions only the owner can make

The complete short form is [ANKI_ESTATE_OWNER_DECISIONS.md](ANKI_ESTATE_OWNER_DECISIONS.md). The draft scripts implement the recommended structural choices and refuse mismatches rather than silently improvising.

1. **Root labels:** approve `DE German`, `ES Spanish`, `FR French`, `IT Italian`, `PT Portuguese`, `ZH Mandarin` (`code-plus-english`).
2. **Structural labels:** approve the shared numbered English lanes while retaining native unit leaves (`numbered-english`).
3. **Personal errors:** approve moving the five native `9 … errors` clusters to `6 My Errors`.
4. **Dormant root:** approve `zz Dormant`.
5. **Surface collisions:** choose `defer-to-hub-manifest` (recommended evidence tags only) or `keep-all` (no group tags).
6. **`EXPERIMENTS-YT`:** choose `suspend_and_demote` (recommended) or `keep`.

The Expression Hub's four separate choices—normally six initial examples with no hard cap, Balanced weakness policy, the dedicated diagnosis subdeck, and the vertical comic rail—were all accepted on 2026-08-07. They are recorded here as upstream contracts, not reopened estate decisions.

No apply run—even on a disposable clone—should be interpreted as approval for a live run.

## Live copy-back cutover (owner-authorized 2026-08-07)

The owner authorized one live estate migration ahead of the Expression Hub,
explicitly accepting the plan's "unsafe gap": the archived expression tasks
stay suspended with `2 Expression Focus` empty until the Hub ships. The
authorization carried three conditions, all recorded here as satisfied or
binding:

1. **Activity gate on the archived set (satisfied).** Measured on the frozen
   12:47 copy: of the 6,191 cards, 5,578 (90%) were never reviewed; 613 ever
   reviewed (937 lifetime reps, 50 mature). In the trailing 30 days the owner
   made 11,508 reviews collection-wide, of which exactly 2 touched this set
   (last on 2026-07-19; last meaningful activity 2026-06-20). The set is the
   superseded didactic Cloud/Idiom/Phrase family, abandoned since the
   pipeline redesign. Condition met; all phases run as designed, including
   the suspensions.
2. **Deadline.** The full cutover — including the deliberate full AnkiWeb
   upload and a verified clean pull on the iPad — must complete at least one
   day before the owner's 2026-08-10 departure, or the live run is parked
   until they return. Builder-constant deploys follow the same window.
3. **Presence.** The owner gates every phase in person. No phases during the
   night image-mining window (01:30–09:00 local); the add-on import timer is
   paused for the entire window (never run migration and an import
   concurrently).

### Why copy-back

The script guard (`anki_reorg_scripts/_common.py::validated_copy_path`) is
intentionally not overridable: it refuses live-profile paths, paths outside
`anki_reorg_work/`, symlinks, hard links (inode-compared against the live
candidates), and WAL sidecars. The live run therefore uses the guards as
designed rather than bypassing them: mutate a **physical copy** inside the
work area, verify it, then swap the verified file into the live profile
while Anki is closed. At no point is the live collection file a script
target.

### Rehearsal evidence (2026-08-07)

Both drills live in the gitignored work area `docs/research/anki_reorg_work/`:

- `rehearsal_A/` — phases 1–9 dry+apply on a SHA-verified clone of the
  frozen backup; every count landed on the plan's frozen values (205 shells;
  9,894 tag assignments; 26,704 moves incl. 20,513 Fluency; 5,391 family
  moves; 38,322 cards over 19 renames, zero residual archive moves; 12,667
  audio; 5,297 collision tags; 27 experiment cards; 717 shells removed).
  `10_verify.py` PASS with all sacred invariants. Kept as inspection
  evidence.
- `rehearsal_B/` — same phases applied, then `rollback.py` newest-to-oldest
  through the whole chain; all nine journals durably `rolled_back`, and the
  rolled-back collection is logically identical to pristine (notes, cards,
  revlog, deck-catalog fingerprints match).

### Procedure

0. **Owner phase 0**: AnkiWeb sync clean (no pending conflict); File →
   Create Backup and copy the newest `.colpkg` out of the backups dir to
   `/srv`; pause the add-on import timer (it stays paused until the builder
   constants are deployed and one rebuild per family is verified); quit Anki
   cleanly; confirm no nonzero `collection.anki2-wal`/`-journal` sidecar
   remains beside the live collection.
1. **Copy out**: physically copy (never link) the live `collection.anki2`
   into `docs/research/anki_reorg_work/live_cutover_<UTC-ts>/collection.anki2`;
   record SHA-256 of source and copy (must match; `nlink=1`).
2. **Fresh evidence pass on that copy**: `00_inventory`, `generate_deck_map`,
   `analyze_duplicates` (the collision manifest is frozen against THIS copy —
   the 12:47 rehearsal counts are not binding and every count is recomputed);
   `owner_decisions.json` copied from `odd_decisions.approved.json`.
3. **Phases 1–9**: each phase dry-run → owner inspects the printed diff at
   the terminal → apply. Journals stay beside the copy forever. Then
   `10_verify.py` against the phase-1 baseline journal must PASS.
4. **Swap**: with Anki still closed, rename the live `collection.anki2` to
   `collection.anki2.pre-estate-<ts>` (kept indefinitely — this file IS the
   live-step rollback), copy the migrated file into its place, and SHA-verify
   the installed file equals the migrated work-area file. The media directory
   is untouched (the migration changes no media reference).
5. **Owner checkpoint in Anki**: open Anki (imports still paused), confirm
   the tree, study a few known decks. Any mismatch → close Anki, swap the
   preserved original back, and diagnose only on copies.
6. **Sync**: deliberate full "Upload to AnkiWeb" once; then a verified clean
   pull on the iPad before anything else proceeds.
7. **Builders**: change every deck-name constant in one commit (plan
   § Pipeline and add-on compatibility is the inventory), suite green,
   deploy, force one rebuild per family, verify the next imports land inside
   the new tree, and only then unpause the add-on import timer. Any old-name
   shells recreated by a straggler import are removed manually under phase-9
   semantics (empty shells only).
